We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [183] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS（注意力-嵌入梯度隔离盾），一种轻量级防御方法，通过三个反向传播操作关闭联邦学习中梯度反演攻击的三个信息泄漏通道（注意力输出投影梯度、嵌入梯度行范数稀疏性、MLP扩展梯度），在不改变模型架构的情况下将令牌恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中梯度反演攻击能近乎精确地恢复私有训练文本，对协作模型训练构成严重威胁。现有防御方法最多只能处理其中一个泄漏通道，要么损害模型效用，要么留下其他结构性信号，缺乏一种能全面封闭所有泄漏通道的轻量级防御方案。

Method: AEGIS通过分析Transformer梯度结构识别了三个令牌信息泄漏通道：Channel 1来自注意力输出投影梯度的低秩子空间（编码输入嵌入）、Channel 2来自嵌入梯度行范数稀疏性（揭示令牌存在性）、Channel 3来自MLP扩展梯度的可恢复子空间信号。防御由三个反向传播操作组成：冻结注意力投影参数消除Channel 1、向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声破坏Channel 2、向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块噪声掩盖Channel 3。相同的掩蔽梯度驱动本地优化器和服务器导出，不保留任何干净信号。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上的评估显示，AEGIS针对分析型和优化型梯度反演攻击均能将令牌恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或改善模型效用。对Channel 1和Channel 2提供了形式化保证，并针对完全了解防御机制的适应性对手进行了经验验证。

Conclusion: AEGIS是一种有效的、轻量级且无需架构更改的全面防御方案，能够同时封闭所有三个梯度信息泄漏通道，为联邦学习中的文本隐私保护提供了新的解决方案，并具有理论保证和广泛的经验验证。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [184] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双重混淆方法，通过联合混淆更新信息和训练图像来增强联邦学习对图像恢复攻击的鲁棒性，该方法结合了基于随机二值权重的梯度扰动技术与图像加密技术，在ViT图像分类任务上有效降低了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，且不带来超出图像加密本身的分类性能额外损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，攻击者可以从共享的模型更新中重建训练数据，现有的单一防御手段（如梯度扰动或图像加密）保护能力有限，需要更鲁棒的防御方案。

Method: 提出双重混淆方法：(1) 基于随机二值权重的鲁棒性增强技术，将部分梯度元素随机置零以减少攻击者可获取的原始梯度信息；(2) 图像加密技术，降低重建图像的视觉可解释性。两种技术互补。图像加密支持每个客户端和每张图像使用独立密钥，无需显式密钥共享。

Result: 在基于Vision Transformer（ViT）的图像分类任务上，该方法在评估设置下减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，同时未造成超出图像加密本身所带来的分类性能额外退化。

Conclusion: 尽管该组合方法不提供绝对的安全保证，但实验结果表明，将梯度修改与图像加密相结合对隐私增强的联邦学习具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [185] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGL-APT是一个基于信息瓶颈节点的自适应APT调查框架，通过图蒸馏减少冗余、自适应时序图学习持续精炼核心节点、跨时空指纹对齐关联碎片化攻击活动，在DARPA E3数据集上取得了最高95.7%的F1分数，同时相比KAIROS将训练时间、检测延迟和内存使用分别降低了约39%、33%和22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APTs具有隐蔽性和多阶段性，传统的检测方法难以应对；虽然溯源图提供了丰富的攻击调查上下文，但攻击相关证据稀疏且隐藏在海量常规系统活动中，全图学习既计算开销大又难以在长攻击序列上进行关联。

Method: TGL-APT由三个互补组件构成：(1) 信息瓶颈引导的图蒸馏，抑制溯源冗余同时限制结构失真并保持因果可达性；(2) 自适应时序图学习，随着节点相关性演化持续精炼核心节点集；(3) 跨时空攻击指纹对齐，关联不同实体和时间窗口中的碎片化可疑活动。最后通过因果扩展和阶段刻画重构连贯的攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上分别取得95.7%、90.9%和88.9%的F1分数，相比KAIROS将训练时间、检测延迟和内存使用分别降低约39%、33%和22%。

Conclusion: TGL-APT有效平衡了检测性能、计算效率和调查能力，为基于溯源图的APT分析提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [186] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将勒索软件部署建模为基于搜索的软件工程优化问题，利用遗传算法在系统活动统计偏差的硬约束下优化数据加密过程，使攻击模式能够规避行为监控和指纹识别技术，实现长时间潜伏攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件的大规模加密操作容易被现代防御系统即时检测，而现代威胁需要更长的潜伏时间（数小时而非数分钟），存在持久性缺口需要解决。

Method: 将勒索软件执行定义为基于搜索的软件工程优化问题，采用遗传算法作为核心优化方法，在系统活动统计偏差的硬约束条件下优化数据加密策略，生成能够规避行为监控的进化攻击模式。

Result: 实验证明进化出的攻击模式能够成功规避基于指纹技术的行为监控系统，验证了搜索优化方法在生成逃避性恶意软件方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于搜索的方法为生成规避性恶意软件提供了强大框架，对自动化软件防御提出了新的挑战，需要防御方关注这一新兴威胁形态。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [187] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MaliciousSkillBench，一个整合13个公开来源、包含9,740个Skill（7,505恶意+2,235良性）的恶意Agent Skill检测基准，系统评估了多种检测方法并揭示跨源泛化不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills作为可复用指令包，为恶意行为提供了直接分发渠道。然而现有恶意Skill数据集分散在不同来源、工件格式、证据体系及良性覆盖中，且存在重复和结构相关内容，难以直接聚合和评估，缺乏统一的综合基准。

Method: 整合13个公开来源，归一化8,414条原始恶意记录为7,539个标准化唯一身份和4,588个操作结构家族；经过冲突标签排除后构建含9,740个Skill的主基准；对4,983个恶意身份协调11类攻击类别；评估三种学习型文本检测器和三种现成Skill扫描器，并引入Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint三种评估范式。

Result: 学习型检测器在Random评估下达到0.882-0.932的Macro-F1，但在Source-Disjoint评估下仅0.653-0.665；最佳TF-IDF SVM在三种设置下得分0.932/0.916/0.665，保留95.6%恶意召回但良性误报率达62.4%。现成扫描器在降低误报时严重牺牲恶意召回。

Conclusion: 可靠的恶意Skill检测需要更广泛的跨源基准覆盖，以及同时衡量攻击检测能力和良性误报率的联合评估框架。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [188] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ShieldFS，一个符合POSIX标准的文件系统，在机密计算威胁模型下为持久存储提供端到端的完整性和新鲜度保证，无需修改应用程序，并通过扩展ZFS实现为ShieldZFS，在提供强安全保证的同时保持与最先进文件系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算通过TEE保护应用程序，但持久存储仍然脆弱。即使使用磁盘加密，恶意的云提供商仍可对磁盘状态进行回滚、重放、分叉或篡改，破坏有状态应用所需的完整性和新鲜度保证。现有方案要么假设可信存储，要么开销过高，要么将完整性逻辑推入应用程序。

Method: ShieldFS使用简洁的密码学承诺来表示允许的文件系统状态，这些承诺保存在TEE内部并在轻量级可信注册表中复制。磁盘上的数据结构（包括预写日志和存储池）通过哈希链和内嵌Merkle树进行认证。利用事务和写时复制（copy-on-write）原子地更新持久文件系统状态和承诺。在读取时验证承诺，确保即使整个I/O栈不可信也能检测到回滚、重放和歧义攻击。

Result: 通过扩展ZFS实现了ShieldZFS，并用标准文件系统基准测试和真实工作负载进行评估，结果显示ShieldZFS在提供强大的完整性和新鲜度保证的同时，性能与最先进的文件系统相当。

Conclusion: ShieldFS成功地在机密计算环境下为持久存储提供了无需应用修改的端到端完整性和新鲜度保护，证明了在不可信I/O栈场景下实现安全且高性能文件系统的可行性。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [189] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShadowPath提出了一种将凭证状态查询转移到持有者端的可撤销可验证凭证方案，通过零知识证明让验证者仅获知撤销状态结果而不泄露可链接的元数据。作者首次评估了Verkle树在凭证撤销中的应用，并与稀疏Merkle树进行对比，实验表明更短的认证路径并不必然带来更廉价的零知识证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可验证凭证撤销状态检查可能暴露重复标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可被用作稳定句柄来关联不同的凭证展示，从而破坏持有者的隐私模型。

Method: 将凭证状态查询从验证者端转移到持有者端，持有者通过零知识证明（Groth16）证明凭证在验证者选定的注册表根下未被撤销。作者对Verkle树（基于KZG多项式承诺）和稀疏Merkle树进行了详细的实验对比评估。

Result: 30次桌面实验中，稀疏Merkle树的Groth16证明中位耗时为371.6ms，Verkle树为2.11s；验证分别耗时3.70ms和7.55ms。两款主流移动设备上Verkle证明约需3秒。

Conclusion: 更短的认证路径并不一定带来更便宜的零知识证明；在使用新鲜的会话随机数时，验证者可见的状态数据在会话值独立性假设下不会泄露两次展示是否使用同一凭证，但该保证不涵盖发行者与验证者共谋及同步流量场景。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root. The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [190] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个标准化的指令型LLM分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（包括边界案例），在受控网络安全测试台上生成端点特定数据，评估了三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B、GPT-OSS 20B）与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch异常检测的性能。Meta Llama表现最佳（准确率89.3%，F1 91.8%），显著优于传统方法的52.0%和49.3%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常行为日志检测方法存在局限：Wazuh等基于预定义规则的方法僵化，OpenSearch等统计方法缺乏语义理解；而LLM方法受提示构造、噪声数据和通用数据集缺乏端点特定认证行为的影响。为解决这些限制，需要开发更有效的检测框架。

Method: 构建标准化指令型LLM分类框架，在受控网络安全测试台上生成包含正常、边界和异常场景的端点特定认证数据集，使用统一基础真值严重性框架，评估三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B）与传统Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch异常检测进行对比。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct表现最佳：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%、假阴性率11.8%，检测到80%的边界异常场景。相比之下Wazuh准确率52.0%、假阴性率68.6%；OpenSearch准确率49.3%、假阴性率74.5%。Qwen推理延迟最低且结构化响应有效性100%，GPT-OSS在有效响应时表现出较强分类性能。

Conclusion: LLM方法在异常认证行为检测上显著优于传统规则和统计方法，尤其在边界案例的识别方面优势明显，证明了LLM语义理解能力在日志异常检测领域的实用价值。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [191] [Tracking the Trend in How Speech Synthesizers Deceive People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19959)
*Milan Šalko,Anton Firc,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Martin Perešini,Filip Pleško,Jakub Reš*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了人类对现代语音合成工具所生成深度伪造音频的检测能力。研究发现，对于完全合成的语音，人类检测的F1分数从RTVC和YourTTS的约90%骤降至ElevenLabs的48%；而对于部分伪造（仅篡改句子中的一句话），人类检测的严格准确率仅9%，且77%的情况下会将合成的句子误判为真实语音。人类与预训练检测器在失败方式上具有互补性，但都无法可靠定位短时篡改。此外，听众越来越倾向于将真实语音误标为伪造，削弱了对未篡改音频的信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期研究虽然报告了70-80%的人类检测准确率，但这些研究主要基于较老的语音合成器。随着语音合成技术（特别是2022年和2024年发布的工具）的飞速进步，现有的人类检测能力评估已经过时，需要重新审视人类在面对现代深度伪造音频时的实际检测能力。

Method: 选取2019年（RTVC）、2022年（YourTTS）和2024年（ElevenLabs）发布的三种语音合成工具，招募82名IT专业人员参与人工检测实验，并将其检测结果与六种预训练深度伪造检测器在同一材料上的表现进行对比基准测试。实验涵盖完全合成语音（full spoofs）和部分伪造（partial spoofing）两种场景。

Result: 完全合成语音场景下，人类检测F1分数从RTVC和YourTTS的约90%降至ElevenLabs的48%；部分伪造场景下，严格准确率仅9%，77%的情况下将合成句误判为真实。人类与检测器的失败方式互补，但都无法可靠定位短时篡改。听众还越来越多地将真实语音误判为伪造。

Conclusion: 人类感知在应对现代和部分伪造条件下已不可靠，这凸显了程序化验证、来源认证、水印技术和片段级检测的必要性，以弥补人类和现有自动检测手段的不足。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake audio highly realistic. Earlier studies reported 70-80% human detection accuracy, but relied primarily on older synthesizers. We compare human detection for three selected voice synthesis tools released in 2019, 2022, and 2024 with 82 IT professionals, and benchmark humans against six pretrained detectors on the same material. For fully synthetic speech (full spoofs), the F1 score drops from about 90% for RTVC and YourTTS to 48% for ElevenLabs, although listeners were explicitly warned that deepfakes were present. For partial spoofing, where only one sentence of an utterance is altered, strict accuracy falls to 9%, and listeners classify the synthetic sentence as bona fide 77% of the time. Humans and detectors fail in complementary ways, and neither reliably localizes short manipulations. Additionally, listeners increasingly mislabel bona fide speech as fake, eroding trust in unmanipulated audio. These findings show that human perception alone is unreliable for the selected modern and partial-spoof conditions and motivate procedural verification, provenance, watermarking, and segment-level detection.

</details>


### [192] [COPA: Continual Preference Optimization for Adaptive Prompt Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19982)
*Roshan Sood,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出COPA框架，将提示注入防御视为终身学习问题，通过GRPO优化和带权重的经验回放实现持续偏好优化，在不断演化的攻击分布中既能适应新威胁又能保持对旧攻击的防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM提示注入防御多为静态方法，依赖固定对齐目标或针对特定攻击的过滤机制，无法应对持续演化的自适应攻击者。虽然已有终身对齐方法处理用户偏好变化，但未考虑对抗性攻击者的持续演进，实际部署中需要在不牺牲旧防御能力的前提下持续适应新威胁。

Method: COPA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于GRPO的持续优化，从新观测到的攻击中增量获取反馈；(2) 边际加权经验回放（margin-weighted experience replay），用于保留对先前攻击类别的防御能力，缓解灾难性遗忘并保持通用能力。

Result: 在终身提示注入攻击流上，与最先进防御方法相比，COPA将攻击成功率平均降低最高6.3倍和4.4倍。

Conclusion: 持续偏好优化是防御LLMs免受自适应对手攻击的有效范式，能够在不断变化的威胁环境中同时实现持续适应、抵御遗忘和保持通用模型能力。

Abstract: LLMs remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, where adversarial instructions embedded in user inputs or external content manipulate model behavior and bypass safeguards. Existing defenses are predominantly static, relying on fixed alignment objectives or attack-specific filtering mechanisms that require redesign as new attack strategies emerge. While recent lifelong alignment methods address shifting user preferences, they do not account for adaptive adversaries that continually evolve to exploit weaknesses in previously learned defenses. This limitation is particularly important in real-world deployments, where evolving attack distributions necessitate continual adaptation without sacrificing robustness to previously encountered threats. We present COPA, a continual preference optimization framework that treats prompt-injection defense as a lifelong learning problem. Instead of one-time alignment, COPA incrementally incorporates feedback from newly observed attacks via GRPO-based optimization and uses margin-weighted experience replay to retain defenses against prior attack classes. This enables continuous adaptation to emerging threats while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and preserving general-purpose model capabilities. Across lifelong prompt injection attack streams, COPA reduces attack success rate by up to 6.3x and 4.4x on average compared to state-of-the-art defenses. These results highlight continual preference optimization as an effective paradigm for defending LLMs against adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [193] [EchoCoT: Extracting Hidden Chain-of-Thought from Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20055)
*Yiting Qu,Ziqing Yang,Chi Cui,Ye Leng,Junjie Chu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoCoT攻击方法，通过API交互从黑盒大型推理模型中近乎逐字提取隐藏的思维链（CoT），在开源模型上达到最高66.4%的提取成功率，并能从Gemini-2.5中提取33463个tokens，揭示了隐藏CoT提取构成现实安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏思维链（CoT）轨迹是前沿专有大型推理模型的重要模型资产，但能否从黑盒模型中直接提取这些隐藏CoT此前未被系统性研究，这一安全漏洞亟待探索。

Method: 识别工具调用之间被忽视的推理重放面，开发EchoCoT多步攻击方法，利用API返回的保真度信号迭代提取隐藏CoT；并构建基于LLM的优化框架，自动搜索跨数据集有效的通用注入轨迹。

Result: 在开源模型上实现最高66.4%的近乎逐字提取成功率，提取轨迹长度与目标差异在10%以内，且至少90%的tokens精确匹配；同一注入轨迹在未见数据集上最高达80%提取成功率；在Gemini-2.5上成功提取33463个tokens（目标32948个）。

Conclusion: 隐藏CoT提取被证实为实际的安全风险，突显了需要更好地保护隐藏CoT资产的重要性。

Abstract: Hidden chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, especially those from frontier proprietary large reasoning models (LRMs), are valuable model assets. Yet whether these hidden CoTs can be directly extracted from black-box models remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically study whether hidden CoTs can be extracted near-verbatim from black-box LRMs through API interactions. We identify a previously overlooked reasoning replay surface between tool calls and develop EchoCoT, a multi-step attack that iteratively extracts hidden CoTs using API-returned fidelity signals. We further develop an LLM-based optimization framework that automatically searches for an effective universal injection trajectory across various datasets. We evaluate EchoCoT on three open-source and five frontier proprietary LRMs. On open-source LRMs, EchoCoT achieves up to 66.4\% near-verbatim extraction success, with the extracted trace length within 10\% of the target and at least 90\% of tokens exactly matching the target CoT. The same injection trajectory also generalizes to unseen datasets, achieving up to 80\% extraction success under the same criterion. For tested frontier proprietary LRMs, a substantial fraction of extracted CoTs closely align with provider-reported reasoning lengths and available CoT summaries. EchoCoT can also extract very long CoTs: on Gemini-2.5, it extracts 33,463 tokens from a 32,948-token target. These results establish hidden-CoT extraction as a practical security risk and highlight the need to better protect hidden CoT assets.

</details>


### [194] [TrustRAG: Blockchain-Enhanced RAG via Committee-Based Credibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20097)
*Baixiang Liu,Haotian Che,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustRAG是一个基于委员会和区块链的检索增强生成系统，通过零知识证明和多方安全计算为文档生成可验证的信任评分，并利用哈希承诺跨链维护文档与评分，确保RAG系统中检索内容的可信性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖集中式数据库且缺乏监督，无法验证文档来源、完整性和可信度。在医疗、金融、物流和法律等领域，错误或被篡改的文档可能导致严重决策失误，因此需要建立可验证、防篡改的信任机制。

Method: TrustRAG采用三管齐下的方案：1) 通过零知识协议由领域专家委员会对文档进行认证；2) 利用安全多方计算将委员会隐藏评分合成为客户端可验证的信任评分；3) 通过哈希承诺将评分和文档数据跨链维护，确保任何文档或评分无法被静默修改或删除，所有排序可独立重放和核查。

Result: 论文提出了TrustRAG系统的完整架构设计，实现了文档可信认证、可验证信任评分和跨链数据完整性保障三大核心能力，使RAG系统的每次检索排序都可以被独立审计和重放验证。

Conclusion: TrustRAG通过结合委员会认证、零知识证明、安全多方计算和区块链技术，为RAG系统提供了端到端的可信保障机制，解决了集中式RAG在来源验证、防篡改和信任评估方面的根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets Large Language Models (LLMs) pull in up-to-date, domain-specific information instead of relying only on what they were trained on. Yet most RAG systems still draw from centralized databases with limited oversight, making it difficult to verify where a document came from, whether it has been tampered with, or whether it should be trusted at all. This is a serious problem in domains where both the timeliness and accuracy of retrieved content are critical, such as healthcare, finance, logistics, and legal case law, where a wrong or manipulated document can directly lead to bad decisions.
  We present TrustRAG, a committee-based, blockchain-backed RAG system: before a document is used, it is certified by a committee of domain experts through a zero-knowledge protocol, and the committee's hidden scores are combined via secure multi-party computation into a trust score that any client can verify. These scores, along with the underlying document data, are maintained jointly across chains through hash commitments, so no document or score can be silently altered or dropped, and every ranking can be independently replayed and checked.

</details>


### [195] [A Meta-Study on Replication Papers in Usable Security & Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20108)
*Christian Mack,Benjamin Berens,Hanna Algedri,Tobias Hilt,Daniela Reimer,Peter Mayer,Melanie Volkamer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法研究了可用安全与隐私领域中复制研究的现状：分析了13个相关venue的征稿要求、系统检索了2016-2025年间24篇复制研究、采用Olszewski等人的复制分类法进行分类，并对作者进行了调查。研究发现：征稿指南需要更清晰、复制研究的修改难以辨别、样本中不存在严格的精确复制，且时间与环境变化是最常见的复制动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用安全与隐私研究是一个年轻且不断发展的领域，尚未建立完善的研究标准（例如关于复制的规范）。该领域缺乏对复制研究现状的系统性了解。

Method: 采用混合方法：（1）审查13个安全、隐私和人机交互venue的Call for Papers；（2）系统检索2016-2025年间报告复制用户研究的论文，获得24篇相关文献；（3）使用Olszewski等人（2025）提出的复制分类法对这些论文进行分类；（4）向论文作者发放调查问卷以了解复制动机。

Result: 研究揭示了四个关键发现：（A）征稿启事需要为作者和审稿人提供更清晰的复制研究指南；（B）阅读复制论文时难以确定相对原始研究做了哪些修改；（C）样本中不存在严格的精确复制，约三分之二的研究修改了原始工作的多个方面；（D）时间和情境变化导致结果改变是最常被引用的复制动机之一。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，作者为venue、研究者和同行评审者提供了实际建议，以加强可用安全与隐私研究中的复制实践。

Abstract: The field of usable security and privacy research is a young and expanding field, which is still developing standards for its research, e.g. regarding replications. We used a mixed-method approach, in order to get a better understanding of the current state of replications in the field of usable security and privacy: (1) we examine the Call for Papers of 13 venues spanning security, privacy, and human-computer interaction; (2) we conduct a systematic search for papers reporting replicated user studies published across these venues between 2016 and 2025, yielding 24 relevant publications; (3) we categorized these 24 papers employing the replication taxonomy proposed by Olszewski et al. (2025); (4) we distributed a survey to the authors of these papers to understand their motivations for conducting replications. Our analysis reveals four key insights: (A) Calls for Papers would benefit from clearer guidelines for authors and reviewers regarding replication work; (B) determining what modifications were made relative to the original study proves difficult when reading replication papers; (C) strict exact replications do not exist in our sample. Approximately two-thirds of the 24 studies altered multiple aspects of the original work; (D) temporal and contextual changes affecting results emerged as one of the most frequently cited motivations for replication. Based on these findings, we offer practical recommendations for venues, researchers, and peer reviewers to strengthen replication practices in usable security and privacy research.

</details>


### [196] [Privacy-Preserving Detection of Rare Disease-Associated Cell Subsets via Secure Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20118)
*Ş. Selcan Magara,Esther Havemann,Debora Jutz,Ali Burak Ünal,Mete Akgün*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，使得CellCnn卷积神经网络能够在秘密共享数据上完成训练和推理，从而在不暴露原始患者数据的前提下，从高维单细胞测量中检测稀有疾病相关细胞亚群。在CMV和AML基准数据集上，该方法在保持接近明文精度的同时，优于先前的隐私保护基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有疾病相关细胞亚群的检测对理解白血病和病毒感染等疾病至关重要，但CellCnn模型的可靠训练需要大规模、多样化的患者队列，远超单一机构所能汇集的范围。同时，单细胞数据过于敏感，在现有隐私法规下无法跨机构共享，因此需要一种既能协作训练又不泄露患者原始数据的隐私保护方案。

Method: 提出一个基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，使CellCnn模型可完全在秘密共享数据上进行训练和推理。该框架确保参与方和计算服务器都无法观察到原始患者数据或中间值。与先前去除ReLU激活函数和偏置项的隐私保护方法不同，本方法完整保留了CellCnn架构中的关键组件。

Result: 在巨细胞病毒（CMV）感染和急性髓系白血病（AML）基准单细胞数据集上，该实现保持了接近明文对应版本的精度，同时优于先前的隐私保护基线方法。

Conclusion: 该MPC框架在保护患者数据隐私的前提下实现了CellCnn的完整训练和推理，保留了模型的完整架构（包括ReLU和偏置项），为跨机构协作分析稀有疾病相关细胞亚群提供了可行的隐私保护解决方案。

Abstract: The detection of rare disease-associated cell subsets from high-dimensional single-cell measurements is critical for understanding diseases such as leukaemia and viral infections. CellCnn, a convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for this task, has demonstrated the ability to identify phenotype-associated cell populations at frequencies as low as 0.01\%. Training such models reliably requires patient cohorts that are larger and more diverse than any single institution can typically assemble, and the underlying single-cell data is too sensitive to share across institutional boundaries under existing privacy regulations. We propose a secure multi-party computation (MPC) framework that enables the training and inference of CellCnn entirely on secret-shared data. This ensures that neither the participants nor the computing servers ever observe raw patient data or intermediate values. Evaluated on benchmark single-cell datasets for cytomegalovirus infection (CMV) and acute myeloid leukaemia (AML), our implementation preserves accuracy close to its plaintext counterpart while outperforming the prior privacy-preserving baseline. In contrast to earlier privacy-preserving approaches that removed components such as ReLU activations and bias terms, our method retains these key parts of the CellCnn architecture and supports accurate analysis without exposing raw patient data.

</details>


### [197] [Trustworthy mobile edge caching: a blockchain approach to mitigate malicious nodes and incentivize cache sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20145)
*Motahare Ebrahimi,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Seyedakbar Mostafavi,Fatemeh Pakzaban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区块链的信任管理机制，用于解决移动边缘缓存中的恶意节点识别、自私节点激励和缓存容量不足等问题。通过智能合约计算直接与间接信任值、实施激励机制和认证机制，能够有效过滤恶意节点并促进缓存资源共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络流量持续增长，边缘服务器内容缓存对降低延迟至关重要。然而，恶意边缘服务器可能删除或篡改缓存内容，且边缘服务器容量有限。辅助移动节点虽可贡献缓存资源，但因其自私行为和可能的恶意性，需要有效的信任管理和激励机制来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出基于区块链的信任管理机制，包含以下核心组件：(1) 通过智能合约计算直接和间接信任值，基于移动节点对服务质量的满意度来确定可信度；(2) 信任数据安全存储于区块链；(3) 引入奖励机制激励缓存共享以对抗节点自私行为；(4) 基于区块链的认证机制防止节点冒充攻击；(5) 在共识过程中考虑节点移动性、能量消耗和计算能力约束，优化信任、缓存容量和成本效率。

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使在数据存在10%噪声的情况下，所提方法仍能准确区分诚实与恶意服务器。

Conclusion: 该机制能有效解决移动边缘缓存中的信任管理问题，同时通过激励和认证机制保障系统的安全性和可靠性，实现在动态移动网络环境下对缓存资源的优化利用。

Abstract: As mobile network traffic continues to grow, content caching on edge servers is critical for reducing latency. However, challenges such as malicious edge servers that may delete or manipulate cached content, along with the limited capacity of these servers, need to be addressed. To overcome the capacity limitations, helper mobile nodes can contribute their cache resources. However, due to their selfish behavior, an incentive mechanism is necessary to encourage resource sharing. Additionally, these helper nodes can also be malicious. This paper proposes a blockchain-based trust management mechanism that addresses these challenges by accurately identifying trustworthy edge servers and mobile nodes. The proposed mechanism calculates both direct and indirect trust using smart contracts, ensuring that malicious nodes are effectively filtered out. Trustworthiness is determined based on mobile node satisfaction with the quality of service, and trust data is securely stored on the blockchain. To combat node selfishness, a reward mechanism is introduced to incentivize cache sharing. Furthermore, a blockchain-based authentication mechanism protects against node impersonation. Our approach optimizes trust, cache capacity, and cost efficiency while considering mobile node mobility, energy consumption, and computational power constraints during the consensus process. Simulation results show that the proposed method can accurately distinguish between honest and malicious servers, even with a 10% noise in data.

</details>


### [198] [Chameleon: Robust Defense Against Tor Website Fingerprinting via Many-to-Many Traffic Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20160)
*Yuwen Cui,Kai Wei,Kehan Shen,Ning Wang,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Chameleon，一种基于多对多随机流量变形（many-to-many randomized traffic morphing）的网站指纹（WF）防御方法。通过选择高类内多样性、低类间差异的变形候选，并随机将网页流量映射到多个候选目标，Chameleon增强了对抗者的不确定性。在Tor可插拔传输（PT）环境中通过radix-trie同步机制实现实际部署。实验表明，相比Adaptive Tamaraw，Chameleon在对抗训练攻击精度上降低36.74%，同时将带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%，并显著限制基于防御感知自编码器（DAAE）的攻击性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WF防御大多存在可学习的网页流量映射特征；即使对对抗训练具有鲁棒性，也不一定对DAAE攻击具有鲁棒性，亟需一种更强大的防御方案。

Method: 提出Chameleon防御框架：(1)多对多随机流量变形，候选选择依据高类内多样性、低类间差异；(2)每个网页随机映射到多个候选，不同网页共享变形目标；(3)基于radix-trie的同步机制支持PT端点用分组方向前缀识别一致的变形轨迹；(4)引入轨迹突变和归一化前缀匹配降低开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集、封闭/开放世界场景下对比6种SOTA防御和5种WF攻击。相比Adaptive Tamaraw，对抗训练攻击精度降低36.74%，带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%。在DAAE攻击下，Chameleon将攻击F1限制在35.19%（Adaptive Tamaraw为88.22%）。真实PT桥接评估中仅增加16.25%时间开销。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过多对多随机映射有效提升对抗不确定性，同时大幅降低开销，在现有防御中展现出显著的鲁棒性和实用性优势，且可在真实Tor PT环境中部署。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer users' browsing activities from encrypted Tor traffic by exploiting side-channel features. Although many WF defenses have been proposed, we find that most existing defenses create learnable web trace mapping features. We further show that robustness against adversarial training does not necessarily imply robustness against defense-aware autoencoder (DAAE)-based attacks.
  To address these limitations, we present Chameleon, a robust WF defense based on many-to-many randomized traffic morphing. Chameleon selects morphing candidates with high intra-class diversity and low inter-class disparity. Chameleon randomly maps each webpage trace to multiple candidates, and allows different webpages to share morphing targets, thereby increasing adversarial uncertainty. For practical Tor deployment, Chameleon introduces a radix-trie-based synchronization mechanism that enables pluggable transport (PT) endpoints to identify consistent morphing traces using packet-direction prefixes, together with trace mutation and normalized prefix matching to reduce overhead. We evaluate Chameleon against six state-of-the-art defenses and five WF attacks on three public datasets in closed- and open-world settings. Compared with Adaptive Tamaraw, Chameleon reduces adversarial-training-based attack accuracy by up to 36.74% while reducing bandwidth and time overhead by 34.12% and 60.38%, respectively. Under DAAE-based RF attacks on GTT23, Chameleon limits attack performance to 35.19% F1-score while Adaptive Tamaraw only limits it to 88.22% F1-score. In the real-world PT bridge evaluation, Chameleon substantially reduces the effectiveness of strong WF attacks while incurring only 16.25% time overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [The Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP): A Portable Contract for AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19263)
*Aniket Wattamwar,Manav Anandani,Mrunal Kakirwar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出评估上下文协议（ECP），这是一个早期阶段的供应商中立框架，旨在为智能体系统提供可移植的评估契约层，通过JSON-RPC接口统一智能体输出、工具调用和审计上下文的评估方式，并提供了支持LangChain、LlamaIndex、CrewAI和PydanticAI的开源参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的演进使得评估范式需要从孤立的LLM转向自主智能体架构，但当前评估范式存在严重局限：基准测试被利用、"自信地犯错"现象、理论能力与运维可靠性之间的差距，以及评估基础设施碎片化等问题亟需解决。

Method: 本文提出ECP（Evaluation Context Protocol），定义了一个小型的JSON-RPC接口，通过该接口智能体暴露其用户可见输出、工具调用以及评估者安全的审计上下文，并支持跨框架和CI系统统一运行程序化检查。同时提供了包含LangChain、LlamaIndex、CrewAI和PydanticAI适配器的开源参考实现。

Result: ECP作为工作方案而非最终标准提出，设计了统一的评估接口和审计上下文机制，其评估面、方法集和评分器家族预计将随着协议在更多系统中的实践而不断演进，实证验证被列为未来工作。

Conclusion: ECP是一种早期阶段的可移植评估契约层方案，旨在解决当前智能体评估基础设施碎片化的问题，但仍需通过更多系统的实践验证和实证研究来证明其采用价值。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence has necessitated a fundamental shift from evaluating isolated Large Language Models (LLMs) to assessing autonomous agentic architectures. This paper explores the critical methodologies for evaluating AI agents and the essential role of advanced observability infrastructure. We analyze the architectural components of agents and identify the severe limitations of current evaluation paradigms, including benchmark exploitation, the "confidently wrong" phenomenon, and the discrepancy between theoretical capability and operational reliability. To begin addressing the fragmentation in current evaluation infrastructure, this paper proposes the Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP), an early-stage, vendor-neutral framework intended to act as a portable evaluation contract layer for agentic systems. In its current form ECP defines a small JSON-RPC interface over which an agent exposes its user-visible output, the tool calls it made, and evaluator-safe audit context, and against which programmatic checks can be run uniformly across frameworks and continuous integration systems. We describe an open-source reference implementation that includes adapters for LangChain, LlamaIndex, CrewAI, and PydanticAI, and we situate the design against failure modes documented in the recent literature. ECP is presented as work in progress rather than a finished standard: the evaluation surface, method set, and grader families are all expected to change as the protocol is exercised against more systems, and the empirical validation required to justify adoption is outlined as future work.

</details>


### [200] [Stable Within, Unidentified Across: Semantic Identification of Benchmark Effects and Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19269)
*Xi Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估语义识别"（evaluation-semantic identification）概念，指在声明的语义族上不变的端点Q(s)。通过TraceElephant案例展示：在F_asym族中，F_asym产生-40.76至-43.03个百分点的不对称采纳效应；而F_cc族中两个对比处理均精确为0且置信区间为[0,0]。两者的并集F_audit无法识别T，尽管F_cc可识别T=0。因子审计将全部分歧归因于单侧终点删除，非终点贡献为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估结论依赖于评估者控制的语义选择（法律引用、可评分性、聚合方式），但现有研究缺乏系统化框架来检验结论在语义族上的稳定性，因此有必要提出评估语义识别概念并进行局部非蕴含检验。

Method: 提出工件定义端点Q(s)在声明族上的不变性作为评估语义识别标准；采用冻结的217行分析在受限契约族内验证稳定性；使用因子审计（factorial audit）将分歧分解为终端删除与非终端贡献；构建可复用的族索引审计（family-indexed audit）。

Result: F_asym产生-40.76至-43.03个百分点的任务不相交采纳效应；F_cc中对比处理均精确为零且区间为[0,0]；并集F_audit不识别T但F_cc识别T=0；因子审计揭示100%分歧来自单侧终端删除，非终端贡献为零；排名在对称处理间仍依赖具体规范。

Conclusion: 这是一个局部的非蕴含见证（localized non-implication witness）和可复用的族索引审计，而非流行率估计、穷尽语义分类或独立复制研究。研究强调评估结论的规范敏感性需要明确的语义族声明来界定其适用边界。

Abstract: Evaluation conclusions depend on evaluator-controlled semantics: legal references, scoreability, and aggregation. We call an artifact-defined endpoint Q(s) evaluation-semantically identified when it is invariant over a declared family. A frozen 217-row analysis appears stable within its restricted contract family. In TraceElephant, F_asym yields precise task-disjoint adoption effects from -40.76 to -43.03 percentage points, whereas both jointly frozen contrast-comparable treatments in F_cc yield exactly zero with [0, 0] intervals. Their union F_audit therefore does not identify T, even though F_cc identifies T = 0. A factorial audit attributes 100 percent of the disagreement to one-sided terminal deletion, with zero nonterminal contribution. Rankings remain specification-dependent across the symmetric treatments. This is a localized non-implication witness and reusable family-indexed audit, not a prevalence estimate, exhaustive semantic taxonomy, or independent replication.

</details>


### [201] [Hype Meets Reality: Large Language Models as Mutators in Search-based Automated Program Repair of Simulink-Stateflow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19347)
*Ayesha Irshad,Pablo Valle,Jon Ayerdi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了将大语言模型（LLM）生成的变异算子集成到基于搜索的自动程序修复（APR）中，用于Simulink/Stateflow建模的CPS系统。在19个真实缺陷模型的基准上，LLM驱动的修复性能显著下降（4-6个合理补丁 vs 原方法18个），揭示了LLM在精确符号编辑、行为反馈和搜索空间探索方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的搜索式APR依赖精心设计的变异算子，而LLM具备动态生成修复建议的能力。作者希望探究用LLM替代部分变异算子能否提升CPS（Simulink/Stateflow）领域程序修复的灵活性和表达能力。

Method: 扩展了FlowRepair框架，将其部分变异算子替换为LLM生成的变异，在相同的wall-clock预算和实验设置下，对19个来自四个CPS领域的真实Stateflow缺陷模型进行受控对比评估，并测试多种LLM变体。

Result: LLM-based修复在FlowRepair实验设置下显著退化：仅产生4-6个合理补丁和4个有效补丁，而原始方法分别达到18个和16个。分析显示LLM难以进行精确符号编辑、缺乏行为反馈，且生成的搜索空间噪声大。

Conclusion: 该结果并非表明LLM在APR中普遍无用，而是揭示了将LLM朴素集成到搜索式APR中的根本性局限，并建议采用结合结构化变异与生成式引导的混合方法。

Abstract: Search-based Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques rely on carefully designed mutation operators to explore the space of candidate fixes. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest that generative models could replace such operators by dynamically proposing repairs. In this paper, we investigate this hypothesis in the context of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) modeled in Simulink/Stateflow. We extend the state-of-the-art FlowRepair approach by replacing a subset of its mutation operators with LLM-generated mutations, enabling more flexible and expressive patch generation. We evaluate the approach on a benchmark of 19 real-world faulty Stateflow models across four CPS domains, using the same experimental setup as FlowRepair for controlled comparison under the same wall-clock budget. Contrary to expectations, in this controlled evaluation, the LLM-based mutation substantially degrades repair performance under the FlowRepair experimental setup. Across the tested LLM variants, the LLM-based repair produced plausible patches for 4-6 models and valid patches for 4 models, compared to 18 and 16, respectively, with the original approach. Our analysis reveals that, in this integration, LLMs struggle with precise symbolic edits, lack behavioral feedback, and generate a noisy search space that hinders effective exploration. Rather than showing a general limitation of LLMs for APR, these findings highlight fundamental limitations of naively integrating LLMs into search-based APR and motivate hybrid approaches that combine structured mutation with generative guidance.

</details>


### [202] [RD-Gen: Random DAG Generator Considering Multi-rate Applications for Reproducible Scheduling Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19460)
*Atsushi Yano,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RD-Gen，一个考虑多速率应用的随机有向无环图（DAG）生成器，用于可复现的实时系统调度评估，支持批量生成不同参数的随机DAG集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统日益庞大复杂，DAG模型用于表达任务依赖和并行性，但现有工具无法生成考虑最新多速率应用的随机DAG集合，研究者需自行生成，导致额外工作量且降低可靠性和可复现性。

Method: 提出RD-Gen随机DAG生成器，该工具能够考虑多速率应用场景，并支持批量生成具有不同参数的随机DAG集合，以满足多种问题设置和DAG研究需求。

Result: 通过案例研究证明，RD-Gen能够有效管理各种问题设置和DAG研究需求，验证了其在多速率应用场景下生成随机DAG集合的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: RD-Gen填补了多速率应用场景下随机DAG生成工具缺失的空白，提高了实时系统调度评估的可复现性和可靠性。

Abstract: Real-time systems have various requirements such as the deadline and resource constraints. In addition, real-time systems are becoming larger and more complex, and studies on performance analysis and efficient scheduling algorithms are becoming increasingly important. Directed acyclic graph (DAG) models, which can express task dependencies and parallelism, are used for such studies. Random DAG sets are used to demonstrate the effectiveness and objectivity of methods proposed for real-time systems. However, there is no random DAG generation tool available that can generate a DAG set that considers the latest multi-rate applications. Therefore, researchers need to generate random DAG sets on their own, leading to additional effort and reduced reliability and reproducibility. To solve this problem, we propose a random DAG generator considering multi-rate applications for reproducible scheduling evaluation (RD-Gen). RD-Gen also enables batch generation of random DAG sets with different parameters. Case studies are used to demonstrate that RD-Gen can manage various problem settings and DAG study requirements.

</details>


### [203] [Measuring What a Specification Determines: A Formal Semantic-Block Model and an Execution-Judged Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19475)
*Oleg Grynets,Dmytro Kostetskyi,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的语义块规范模型和执行判定的基准，用于独立于模型能力评估规范质量。该模型包含18个块和19条依赖边，五层分解使每任务上下文减少约71%，覆盖85.5%的Oracle构造分类法。基准采用固定执行者面板和PostgreSQL 16实况Oracle实例作为判定器，六个设计研究揭示确定性作为形式概念成立，但作为独立经验质量指标不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范质量评估往往与模型能力纠缠不清，缺乏独立于执行模型能力的规范评价框架，需要一种形式化的规范和基准来区分规范本身的质量与实施者的能力。

Method: 提出语义块规范模型（含语义块、依赖关系、块属规则、决策点和开放问题），施加四项机器可检查的良构条件；定义模型论意义上的确定性并通过独立实施者的收敛来经验估计；在Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移规范上实例化，通过五层分解、依赖闭包和引用衍生边进行验证。

Result: 五层分解减少约71%的每任务上下文，覆盖85.5%的Oracle构造分类法且缺口已分诊，不被替代分区Pareto支配，可在99.9百分位从引用衍生边恢复。25单元子样本的重复杂跑显示中位臂差14.4个百分点。六个设计研究（含三个预注册操作）表明确定性与经验质量指标之间存在偏差。

Conclusion: 确定性作为形式概念得到支持，但对于评估的当代LLM实施者而言，不能作为独立经验质量指标使用；规范质量评估需要结合形式验证与经验执行。

Abstract: This work introduces a formal semantic-block model for specifications and an execution-judged benchmark for evaluating specification quality independently of model capability. A specification is represented as a structure comprising semantic blocks, dependency relations, block-owned rules, decision points, and explicitly open questions, subject to four machine-checkable well-formedness conditions: acyclicity, single ownership, constraint domination, and totality or ambiguity-stop. Determinacy is defined model-theoretically as agreement among all conforming implementations and is estimated empirically through convergence across independent implementers. The model is instantiated on an Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration specification containing 18 blocks and 19 dependency edges. Computational validation shows that the five-layer decomposition reduces mean per-task context by approximately 71% through dependency closures, covers 85.5% of the study-defined Oracle construct taxonomy with all identified gaps triaged, is not Pareto-dominated by the tested alternative partitions, and is recovered at the 99.9th percentile from citation-derived edges not used to define the original structure. The benchmark keeps the implementer panel fixed, includes a mandatory no-specification control arm, and uses PostgreSQL 16 and a live Oracle instance as deterministic execution judges. Six designed studies, including three pre-registered manipulations and three diagnostic analyses, further examine specification effects. Repeated runs on a 25-unit subsample reveal an empirical variability floor with a median arm-delta spread of 14.4 percentage points. The results support determinacy as a formal concept but not as a standalone empirical quality metric for the evaluated contemporary LLM implementers.

</details>


### [204] [Accelerated Genetic Programming Hyper-Heuristics for Simulation-Based Scheduling via Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19487)
*Heyang Thomas Li,Alexander Pletzer,Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用Claude智能体AI对真实项目调度工作负载进行系统性重构的方法，在HPC环境中将测试运行时间从1,298秒降至200秒以下，且输出不变，每年节省400万核心小时（约32万新西兰元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python科研代码在规模化实验下性能瓶颈严重，尤其在离散事件项目调度模拟中，顺序状态更新、嵌套循环、条件求值和面向对象结构限制了编译数值库和GPU加速库的优势。研究人员往往缺乏时间和专业知识进行底层优化。

Method: 利用Claude agentic AI进行系统性代码重构：以代表性基准测试和正确性检查为指导，智能体识别性能瓶颈、实施针对性优化并评估效果，研究人员保留最终控制权。

Result: 测试运行时间从1,298秒减少到200秒以下，输出结果不变，每年节省四百万核心小时（约32万新西兰元）。

Conclusion: 基于AI智能体的自适应重构方法能有效解决Python科研代码的性能瓶颈问题，兼顾效率提升与结果正确性，且具有显著的经济效益。

Abstract: Python is widely used in scientific research because it enables rapid development and provides rich ecosystems for data analysis, artificial intelligence (AI), and machine learning. However, customized research code can become prohibitively slow as experiments scale. This challenge is particularly acute in discrete-event project-scheduling simulations, where sequential state updates, nested loops, conditional evaluations, and object-oriented structures limit the benefits of compiled numerical and GPU-accelerated libraries. Addressing these bottlenecks typically requires iterative profiling, refactoring, testing, and validation, yet researchers may lack the time or specialized software-engineering expertise for low-level optimization. This paper presents a systematic refactoring approach using Claude agentic AI on real-world project-scheduling workloads in a high-performance computing (HPC) environment. Guided by representative benchmarks and correctness checks, the agent identifies bottlenecks, implements targeted optimizations, and evaluates their effects, while the researcher retains final control. Testing runtime reduced from 1,298 seconds to under 200 seconds without changing outputs, saving four million core-hours (NZ\$320,000) annually.

</details>


### [205] [An Agentic RAG and Evaluation Framework for Assurance Case Generation: Industrial Use Case for the EU Cyber Resilience Act Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19509)
*Fariz Ikhwantri,Iker Lasa Ojanguren,Dusica Marijan,Maria I. Maslioukova,José Arias Marin,Pavlos Kosmides*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于智能体检索增强生成（RAG）的自动化框架，用于为欧盟《网络韧性法案》（CRA）合规生成保障用例（ACs），通过形式化"主张-论证-证据"逻辑将技术文档映射为认证证据，并在Catalink PATROLIoT野火监测系统案例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业（SMEs）在遵守欧盟CRA时面临资源密集型挑战，网络安全合规评估的复杂性高，但合规证明对于展示产品安全性和韧性至关重要。现有方法缺乏自动化且可解释的合规评估方案。

Method: 提出一个自动化框架，采用基于智能体的RAG技术，以形式化的"主张-论证-证据"（Claim-Argument-Evidence）逻辑为基础，系统性地将技术文档需求映射为合规认证证据，生成保障用例（ACs），并引入自然语言推理（NLI）评估器来验证证据的接地性与可追溯性。

Result: 在Catalink的PATROLIoT野火监测系统案例中，框架生成了70个保障用例，每个AC的接地密度约为4.4个工件；NLI评估器达到0.88的准确率，专家验证的可信度得分为3.06。

Conclusion: 该工作为从业者提供了可扩展、可解释的自动化方法，用于强制性的CRA合规评估，在减少人工工作量的同时保持透明的决策支持。

Abstract: Complying with the EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) is a resource-intensive challenge for SMEs due to the complexity of cybersecurity conformity assessment. Yet, it is essential for demonstrating regulatory compliance and ensuring product security and resilience. To address this, we introduce an automated framework for generating Assurance Cases (ACs) using an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation grounded in a formal Claim-Argument-Evidence logic. By systematically mapping technical documentation requirements, the framework streamlines the generation of certification evidence. We validate our approach on a case study of Catalink's PATROLIoT wildfire monitoring system, where the agentic RAG generated 70 ACs with high grounding density (~4.4 artefacts per AC). The proposed Natural Language Inference (NLI) evaluator achieves 0.88 accuracy, which provides robust evidence grounding and traceability, while expert-validated plausibility (3.06) supports interpretable justifications. For practitioners, this work provides a scalable, interpretable approach for automating mandatory CRA conformity assessments, reducing manual effort while maintaining transparent decision support.

</details>


### [206] [Trace-Based Execution-Level Observability of VDM-SL Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19510)
*Tomohiro Oda,Han-Myung Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在VDM规范执行过程中记录赋值、操作调用和返回语句的执行轨迹，使操作内部行为持久化并作为基于状态的模型进行分析，在ViennaTalk中实现了事件数据模型及其可视化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM虽然通过数学定理证明和模拟执行实现了严格验证，但通过解释器进行动画验证时，操作（operation）的内部行为缺乏持久化记录，难以进行深入的分析和可视化，需要一种方法来捕捉和分析执行过程中的内部行为。

Method: 提出记录执行轨迹（execution traces）的方法，包括赋值语句、操作调用和返回语句等事件；设计了执行轨迹中事件的数据模型；在ViennaTalk工具中实现了该模型，并将其应用于可视化展示。

Result: 在ViennaTalk中成功实现了执行轨迹的事件数据模型，并展示了其应用于操作内部行为可视化的效果。

Conclusion: 通过记录和利用执行轨迹，可以将操作的内部行为持久化为基于状态的分析模型，为VDM规范验证提供了更深入的可视化和分析手段。

Abstract: VDM has been pursuing rigorous verification through mathematical theorem proving and software testing via simulated execution. Animation through an interpreter enables validation of the specification to ensure it meets the required functionality. Step-by-step execution in a debugger also allows the user to follow the internal behavior of operations. In this paper, we propose the recording and utilization of execution traces of assignments, operation calls, and return statements to make the internal behavior of operations persistent and analyzable as state-based models. The data model of events in execution traces, its implementation in ViennaTalk, and its application to visualization will be introduced.

</details>


### [207] [Garbage Collection and Energy Consumption in Java: A Controlled Study Across Workloads and JDKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19520)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对Serial、Parallel和G1三种垃圾回收器在三个Java应用、三种工作负载强度和两个JDK发行版（OpenJDK与Oracle JDK）下进行了受控实证评估，发现Parallel平均能耗最低（839.8 J），但统计检验未确认垃圾回收器之间存在显著能耗差异；工作负载强度才是能耗的主要驱动因素，JDK发行版间无显著能耗差异。结论是不存在普遍最优的垃圾回收器，选择应基于目标系统的应用特定测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾回收器的选择是低成本的配置决策，能影响Java应用的性能和能耗，但聚合的能耗排名是否能在异构应用、工作负载强度和JDK发行版间泛化仍不清楚，需要实证研究加以检验。

Method: 采用受控实证实验设计，在三个Java应用、三种工作负载强度和两个JDK发行版（OpenJDK与Oracle JDK）下评估三种垃圾回收器；使用EnergiBridge测量处理器封装能耗和执行时间，并推导互补的能耗-性能指标；采用RCBD ANOVA进行统计分析。

Result: Parallel平均能耗最低（839.8 J），其次是Serial（857.6 J）和G1（969.0 J），但RCBD ANOVA未确认显著的回收器效应；工作负载强度是显著的能耗驱动因素；能耗与执行时间呈中等正相关（r=0.33）；OpenJDK与Oracle JDK之间无显著能耗差异。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍能耗最优的垃圾回收器，工作负载强度是管理Java能耗更可靠的杠杆；垃圾回收器选择应被视为应用特定的调优决策，需在目标系统上通过测量来支持，而非仅依赖聚合排名。

Abstract: Garbage-collector selection is a low-effort configuration decision that can influence both the performance and energy consumption of Java applications. However, it remains unclear whether aggregate energy rankings among collectors generalise across heterogeneous applications, workload intensities, and JDK distributions. This study presents a controlled empirical evaluation of Serial, Parallel, and G1 garbage collection across three Java applications, three workload intensities, and two JDK distributions: OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Using EnergiBridge, processor-package energy consumption and execution time were measured and complementary energy-performance metrics derived. Across all evaluated configurations, Parallel recorded the lowest mean energy consumption (839.8 J), followed closely by Serial (857.6 J) and G1 (969.0 J), but an RCBD ANOVA did not establish a statistically reliable collector effect. Workload intensity, by contrast, was a significant driver of energy consumption, with heavy workloads consuming substantially more energy than light or medium workloads regardless of collector. Energy consumption showed a moderate positive association with execution time (r = 0.33), indicating that longer-running configurations tended toward higher energy use, though the relationship was far from proportional. No statistically significant energy difference was found between OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Overall, the results do not support a universally energy-optimal garbage collector; instead, workload intensity emerges as the more reliable lever for managing Java energy consumption, and collector selection should be treated as an application-specific tuning decision supported by measurement on the target system rather than aggregate rankings alone.

</details>


### [208] [Auditing and Decomposing Feedback-Driven Evolution in LLM Test Generation under the Oracle Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19626)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文审计了反馈驱动的LLM测试生成中"执行反馈作为自验证信号"的可靠性缺陷：当生成输入在单一参考程序上运行并以其输出作为ground truth时，无效或欠指定输入会造成虚假缺陷检测和虚假进化收益。通过142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个留出任务的大规模实验，发现单一参考oracle会使进化测量虚增9.46-14.85个百分点，且真实的三轮反馈循环与安慰剂对照相比并无稳健的细粒度收益证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将执行反馈视为LLM生成测试的自验证信号，但单一参考程序作为ground truth存在系统性漏洞——无效或欠指定的输入会在单一参考上产生虚假的缺陷检测和虚假的进化收益。作者旨在审计这一失效模式并建立更可靠的评估协议。

Method: 设计了大规模审计实验：使用142个开发任务、114个锁定外部任务和138个留出任务，搭配两个代码模型、三个随机种子和故障交叉拟合的真实提交。通过三种被接受实现组成的panel对生成输出进行交叉验证，比较单一参考oracle与panel一致性；引入真实的三轮反馈循环与密度匹配的安慰剂对照；由两位软件工程博士生进行盲法语义审计。

Result: 外部输入中，三种被接受实现一致时，生成输出仅有27.79%和50.12%与panel匹配。单一参考oracle使进化测量虚增9.46-14.85个百分点；审计后，等预算独立重采样优于基于突变的进化达6.01-18.83个百分点。真实反馈与安慰剂的外部差异为+0.13和-0.50分，留出差异为+1.99和+0.28分，无稳健反馈收益证据。盲法审计分类94.41%的panel不一致输入为无效，3.60%为有效。

Conclusion: 单一参考执行反馈存在系统性验证伪影，panel不一致具有信息量但不等同于语义证据。作者提出"审计+安慰剂"协议，将验证器伪影、交互脚手架和grounded反馈信用分离，用于自进化测试生成器的可靠评估。

Abstract: Execution feedback is often treated as a self-verifying signal for improving LLM-generated tests. However, when generated inputs are executed on a single accepted program and its outputs are used as ground truth, invalid or underspecified inputs can create spurious fault detections and apparent evolutionary gains. We audit this failure mode in feedback-driven test generation using 142 development tasks, 114 locked external tasks, and 138 held-out tasks, with two code models, three seeds, and fault-cross-fitted real submissions. On external inputs for which three accepted implementations agree, generated outputs match the panel on only 27.79% and 50.12% of cases. A single-reference oracle inflates the measured gain from evolution by 9.46-14.85 percentage points; after auditing, equal-budget independent resampling outperforms mutation-based evolution by 6.01-18.83 points. We further compare a genuine three-round feedback loop with a density-matched placebo. External Real-Placebo differences are +0.13 and -0.50 points, while held-out differences are +1.99 and +0.28 points and do not provide robust evidence of fine-grained feedback benefit. A blinded semantic audit by two software engineering doctoral students classifies 94.41% of panel-disconfirmed inputs as invalid but 3.60% as valid, showing that panel disagreement is informative but not semantic proof. We propose an audit-and-placebo protocol that separates verifier artifacts, interaction scaffolding, and grounded feedback credit in evaluations of self-evolving test generators.

</details>


### [209] [Loreley: Repository-Scale Program Evolution with Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19703)
*Mohan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Loreley，一种将完整仓库状态保留在质量-多样性（QD）存档中，并将其作为父代或上下文用于后续编辑的智能体搜索框架。通过Zstandard基准的受控实验（1008个候选作业）对比QD、顺序冠军编辑和独立根提议三种策略，结果显示QD在48个作业时比顺序冠军低0.135%，比独立根高0.320%，但均无统计显著性差异，未能证明QD的端点优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序智能体搜索（sequential agent search）会丢弃非冠军分支，缺乏多样性；独立提议（independent proposals）虽保持广度但每次从头开始。现有方法无法兼顾搜索的连续性与多样性，因此需要一种既能保留中间状态、又能在不同分支间跳转的搜索机制。

Method: Loreley在QD存档中保留完整的仓库状态（Git提交），并从中采样父代或作为上下文供后续编辑使用。候选在隔离的worktree中生成，由项目提供的评估器评判。实验设计为Zstandard匹配基准的七组配对块，每策略每块48个物理候选作业，共1008个作业，采用根初始化与各策略原生并发；验证集检查点选胜者，隐藏集评估固定候选。

Result: 在48个作业时，QD比顺序冠军低0.135%（95% BCa区间-0.556%至+0.161%），比独立根高0.320%（区间-0.082%至+0.686%），均无显著差异；顺序冠军48作业均值和中位数最高。四项最终QD胜者的主父系谱中存在非现任状态（在追溯性单现任规则下）；加入灵感边后升至六项。另有三次早期能力战役在两个Python库及独立Zstandard修订中产生第4代多文件改进。

Conclusion: Loreley确实触发了预期的踏脚石（stepping-stone）机制——存档保留和后续采样确实发生，部分获胜者父系中包含非现任状态；但在本受控实验中，QD在48个作业的预算下未能展现出优于顺序冠军或独立根的端点收益。

Abstract: Sequential agent search accumulates changes from its current champion but discards alternative branches; independent proposals preserve breadth but restart from the root. Loreley instead retains complete repository states in a Quality-Diversity (QD) archive and samples them as parents or supplies them as context for later edits. Candidates are Git commits produced in isolated worktrees and judged by a project-supplied evaluator. We compare configured Loreley QD, sequential champion editing, and independent root proposals in a matched Zstandard experiment: seven paired blocks and 48 physical candidate jobs per policy and block (1,008 total), with root-only initialization and each policy's native concurrency. Validation selected a winner at each budget checkpoint; an agent-hidden holdout measured the fixed candidate. At 48 jobs, QD was 0.135% below Sequential Champion (95% BCa interval for the paired effect: -0.556% to +0.161%) and 0.320% above Independent Root (-0.082% to +0.686%). Neither contrast established a QD advantage; Sequential had the highest observed 48-job mean and median. Archive retention and later sampling did occur. Four of seven final QD winners had a non-incumbent state in their primary-parent ancestry under a retrospective one-incumbent rule applied only to the observed QD stream. Including inspiration edges raised the count to six, without showing that supplied context caused an edit. Three earlier capability campaigns produced generation-4, multi-file improvements in two Python libraries and a separate Zstandard revision. Loreley engaged the intended stepping-stone mechanism, but the controlled experiment did not show an endpoint benefit at 48 jobs.

</details>


### [210] [A Fully Automated, Deployment-Aware Testing Pipeline for IoT-Based Automotive Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19752)
*Denesa Zyberaj,Roman Vintonyak,Pascal Hirmer,Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向物联网汽车应用端到端的、面向部署的测试流水线，结合LLM/VLM辅助的需求驱动测试与代码生成，并通过Eclipse openDuT支持跨地理位置的分布式部署。在儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）案例研究中实现了9个需求的完整功能覆盖和100%的Gherkin生成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆嵌入式软件测试面临系统复杂、架构分散、安全性能约束严格等挑战，传统手动测试方法效率低且一致性差，亟需自动化且支持分布式部署的测试方案。

Method: 提出端到端测试流水线，集成需求驱动测试和代码生成，利用大语言模型（LLM）和视觉语言模型（VLM）辅助生成，并结合人在回路（human-in-the-loop）的策展机制减少手动工作量。使用Eclipse openDuT支持在跨地理位置的网络物理系统和物联网基础设施上进行灵活分布式部署，优化节点可用性和跨组织协调。

Result: 在儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）案例研究中，实现了所有9个需求的完整功能覆盖率，可控需求集上Gherkin生成准确率达到100%。通过Eclipse openDuT在跨地理位置ECU上的分布式测试执行验证了该流水线在OEM-供应商测试工作流中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该测试流水线有效降低了手动测试工作量并提高了测试一致性，通过分布式部署能力适配了现代汽车OEM与供应商之间的跨组织测试协作场景，具有实际工业应用价值。

Abstract: Testing embedded software in modern vehicles is challenging due to system complexity, decentralized architectures, and strict safety and performance constraints. In this work, we present an end-to-end, deployment-aware testing pipeline for IoT-based automotive applications. The pipeline combines requirement-driven test and code generation with large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) assistance, and human-in-the-loop curation to reduce manual effort and improve consistency. Using Eclipse openDuT, it supports flexible, distributed deployment across geographically separated cyber-physical and IoT infrastructures, optimizing for node availability and cross-organizational coordination. For validation, we conduct a case study using a Child Presence Detection System (CPDS), achieving full functional requirement coverage across all 9 requirements and 100% Gherkin generation accuracy on the controlled requirement set. Distributed test execution across geographically separated ECUs via Eclipse openDuT confirms the pipeline's applicability to OEM--supplier testing workflows.

</details>


### [211] [PRAXIS: Graph-Grounded Tacit Knowledge for Domain Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19784)
*Xue Jiang,Tianyu Zhang,Lingwei Wu,Ziyu Wang,Ge Li,Yuan Sui,Hao Zhu,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PRAXIS框架，通过从代码库中系统性地提取、表示和复用隐性知识（tacit knowledge），解决LLM智能体在领域特定代码生成上的困难。PRAXIS模拟人类开发流程获取隐性知识，将其结构化存储于代码依赖图中，并在代码交互时主动提供给智能体。实验表明其优于现有SOTA智能体、经验型与技能型方法，且能与各类智能体框架和LLM无缝集成并持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在通用软件工程任务上表现优异，但在领域特定代码生成上表现不佳。根本原因是智能体缺乏开发者通过实践内化但从未文档化的隐性知识（如业务规则、接口契约和操作惯例），这些知识深埋在领域代码及其依赖关系中，难以检索和学习。

Method: PRAXIS框架包含三个关键环节：1) 通过在目标代码库中模拟人类开发工作流程来获取隐性知识；2) 将获取的知识提炼为结构化单元并组织在代码依赖图上；3) 在代码交互点主动将相关知识呈现给智能体供其复用。

Result: 大量实验表明PRAXIS优于配备强大智能体搜索能力的SOTA智能体、基于经验的方法和基于技能的方法。该框架能无缝集成到各类智能体框架和LLM中，持续带来一致性的性能提升，并支持持续进化——随着实践积累，性能稳定增长。

Conclusion: PRAXIS通过系统性地提取和复用隐性知识，有效解决了LLM智能体在领域特定代码生成中的知识缺口问题，提供了一种可扩展、可集成且可持续改进的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents have achieved strong performance on general software engineering tasks, yet struggle with domain-specific code generation. We identify the root cause as the agent's lack of tacit knowledge, including domain-specific business rules, interface contracts, and operational conventions that developers internalize through practice but never document. This knowledge is deeply buried beneath the domain code, dispersed across code entities and their dependency relations, and invisible to the agent that lacks it. These properties make tacit knowledge inherently difficult to retrieve or learn. In this work, we propose PRAXIS, a framework that enables agents to systematically extract, represent, and reuse tacit knowledge for domain code generation. PRAXIS acquires tacit knowledge by simulating human development workflows within the target codebase, distills it into structured units organized on the code dependency graph, and proactively surfaces it to the agent at the point of code interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PRAXIS outperforms state-of-the-art agents equipped with powerful agentic search capabilities, as well as experience-based and skill-based methods. The approach integrates seamlessly into various agent frameworks and LLMs with consistent performance improvements, and supports continual evolution with performance steadily scaling as practice accumulates.

</details>


### [212] [Further Progress Towards Operation Proof Obligation Generation for VDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19848)
*Nick Battle,Carlo Rende,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是对VDM形式化方法中证明义务生成工作的更新，改进了循环不变量、循环变体、操作调用的证明义务（包括隐式声明的操作和规范语句），并为操作添加了递归度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM形式化方法通过证明义务确保模型内部一致性，但现有的证明义务生成方法在处理循环不变量、操作调用和递归等方面存在不足，需要改进以提高形式化验证的准确性和覆盖范围。

Method: 论文提出改进的证明义务生成方法，具体包括：改进了循环不变量的处理方式，增加了循环变体（用于终止性验证），优化了操作调用的证明义务生成（支持隐式声明的操作和规范语句），并为递归操作添加了递归度量。

Result: 通过示例展示了新特性的具体应用，验证了改进后方法在处理循环、操作调用和递归场景下证明义务生成的正确性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了VDM证明义务生成的能力，使其能够更全面、更精确地验证模型的内部一致性，特别是在复杂控制流和递归场景下。

Abstract: The VDM formalism can ensure that models are internally consistent. Potential inconsistencies are highlighted by assertions called proof obligations. This paper is an update to the work described in [1], regarding proof obligation generation for VDM operations. We present the latest work which improves the approach to loop invariants, adds loop variants, improves proof obligations for operation calls, including implicitly declared operations and specification statements, and adds recursive measures for operations. The new features are illustrated with examples.

</details>


### [213] [Repo0: Design-Driven Zero-to-All Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19854)
*Silin Chen,Haoyi Teng,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Jiale Huang,Yongpan Wang,Hongyu Zhang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Repo0，一个面向零到全代码生成的连续结构演化框架，通过维护由需求级DAG、组件级DAG及其对齐关系组成的双DAG架构状态，迭代演化组件边界直至结构收敛，再利用收敛后的架构进行测试驱动代码生成，在多个真实仓库上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在代码生成中大多假设预定义的仓库架构，这不适用于需要从自然语言需求直接构建完整软件项目（零到全代码生成）的场景，因为系统需要在整个开发过程中维持模块化的仓库架构。

Method: Repo0维护一个显式架构状态（Dual-DAG，双有向无环图），包含需求级DAG、组件级DAG及两者对齐关系；从自然语言需求出发，通过结构动作在模块化度量指导下迭代演化组件边界，直至达到结构收敛；随后利用收敛架构指导测试驱动开发（TDD）的代码生成。

Result: 在RepoCraft的六个真实仓库上使用GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek V3.2评估，Repo0在所有设置下取得最高的功能覆盖率和通过率；相比最强基线RPG，功能覆盖率最高提升20.08个百分点，通过率最高提升29.74个百分点；消融实验验证了Dual-DAG架构状态、模块化指导的结构演化及显式结构收敛的重要性。

Conclusion: Repo0证明通过持续的结构演化和显式架构收敛，可以在零到全代码生成任务中有效提升生成软件的质量和模块化程度，为大规模软件项目自动生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents have made substantial progress in code generation, yet most existing systems assume a predefined repository architecture. This assumption does not hold in zero-to-all code generation, where an agent must construct an entire software project directly from natural-language requirements while maintaining a modular repository architecture throughout development. We present Repo0, a continuous structural evolution framework for zero-to-all code generation. Repo0 maintains an explicit architectural state instantiated as a Dual-Directed-Acyclic-Graph (Dual-DAG), consisting of a requirement-level DAG, a component-level DAG, and their alignment relation. Starting from natural-language requirements, it iteratively evolves component boundaries through structural actions guided by modularity metrics until structural convergence, after which the converged architecture guides test-driven development code generation. We evaluate Repo0 on six real-world repositories from RepoCraft using GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek V3.2. Repo0 achieves the highest Functionality Coverage and Pass Rate across all settings. Compared with RPG, the strongest repository-planning baseline, Repo0 improves Functionality Coverage by up to 20.08 percentage points and Pass Rate by up to 29.74 percentage points. Ablation and structural-evolution analyses further demonstrate the importance of the Dual-DAG architectural state, modularity-guided structural evolution, and explicit structural convergence.

</details>


### [214] [Evaluating Automated Testing on an Open-Source Web Application Using Cypress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19960)
*Quoc-Binh Nguyen,Truc-Ly Phan Nguyen,Ngoc Hong Tran,Dung Hai Dinh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于Cypress框架的端到端自动化测试在开源Web应用中的有效性，通过27个测试用例从执行速度、可靠性和可维护性三个维度进行衡量，结果表明测试套件执行时间短且稳定，多数用例结果一致，仅少数涉及复杂交互的测试存在flakiness，同时发现使用data-cy属性的元素定位策略和POM架构能显著降低UI变更时的维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动化测试日益广泛用于Web软件开发，但其实际有效性受执行时间、测试结果稳定性和测试套件可维护性等多种因素影响，需要系统性地评估其在这些维度上的表现。

Method: 以开源Web应用为实验对象，部署基于Cypress框架的端到端自动化测试实验，包含27个测试用例，从执行速度（执行时间和稳定性）、可靠性（多次运行的测试结果一致性）、可维护性（元素定位策略与POM架构的影响）三个维度进行衡量与评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于Cypress的端到端测试套件执行时间短且稳定，适合开发过程中的频繁运行；绝大多数测试用例在多次运行中结果一致，只有少数涉及复杂交互功能的测试出现flakiness；data-cy属性等弹性元素定位策略和POM架构能有效降低UI变化时的维护开销。

Conclusion: Cypress框架的端到端测试在效率、可靠性和可维护性方面整体表现良好，适用于频繁迭代的Web开发环境，但复杂交互场景下的测试稳定性仍需关注，同时合理的元素定位策略和架构设计对降低长期维护成本至关重要。

Abstract: End-to-end automated testing is increasingly used in web software development to ensure system quality and shorten response times during development. However, the true effectiveness of automated testing depends on many factors including execution time, stability of test results, and maintainability of the test suite as the application continues to evolve. In this paper, we evaluate the effectiveness of end-to-end automated testing using the Cypress framework for an open-source web application. We deployed the experiment with 27 test cases. The test's effectiveness is measured by execution speed, reliability, and maintainability. The experimental results show that the Cypress-based end-to-end test suite has short and stable execution times. It suits frequent runs during software development. The majority of test cases achieved consistent results across multiple runs, while flakiness only occurred in a few tests which involve complex interactive functions. Furthermore, the study highlights the impact of element locator strategies and Page Object Model (POM) architecture on test suite maintainability, demonstrating that resilient data-cy attributes significantly reduce maintenance overhead when UI changes occur.

</details>


### [215] [BreakGuard: Towards Detecting Dependency Breaking Changes with LLM-Generated Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20167)
*Rachna Raj,Benoit Baudry,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BreakGuard方法，通过静态提取调用目标库方法的所有客户端焦点方法并为每个方法生成测试，以检测客户端应用程序中的库破坏性变更（BCs）。在BUMP数据集的89个真实BCs上评估，使用GPT4o、Qwen3-coder-480B、GPT-OSS-120B三个LLM和三种上下文级别，最佳配置下检测出30.3%（27/89）的BCs，平均检测成本约0.90美元。研究发现LLM生成测试对崩溃型BC更可靠，对行为型BC效果较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库在演进过程中会引入破坏性变更（BCs），改变运行时行为并破坏客户端应用程序。然而客户端测试套件往往因库覆盖率不足而无法检测到这些BCs，导致问题未被及时发现。现有方法缺乏一种能够系统性地为客户端生成测试来检测库版本更迭引入的破坏性变更的有效方案。

Method: BreakGuard分两步进行：首先静态提取所有调用目标库方法的客户端方法（焦点方法），识别出调用点（call sites）；然后为每个焦点方法生成测试用例。每个测试用于检测BC的依据是：在预破坏版本上通过但在破坏性版本上失败。评估中使用3个LLM（GPT4o、Qwen3-coder-480B、GPT-OSS-120B）和三种上下文级别（最小、方法级、类级）进行配置比较。

Result: 在BUMP数据集的89个真实BCs上评估，最佳配置检测出30.3%（27/89）的破坏性变更，平均每个检测到的BC成本约0.90美元。成功检测了来自不同库类别（如JSON库、日志、解析）的BCs，但LLM生成的测试对崩溃型BC更可靠，对行为型BC的检测效果较差。

Conclusion: BreakGuard是首个系统地利用LLM为客户端生成测试来检测库破坏性变更的方法，证明了LLM生成测试在检测崩溃型BCs方面的有效性，但同时也揭示了在行为型BCs检测上的局限性，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Open-source libraries play an important role in software development by providing reusable features that expedite the development process. As libraries evolve, they release new versions that add features, fix bugs, or apply security patches. In this process, they may break the contract established with their clients by introducing breaking changes (BCs) that alter the runtime behavior and break client applications. Client-side test suites often fail to detect these BCs because of limited library coverage that does not exercise all library methods used in the client's codebase.
  We propose BreakGuard, an approach that generates a test suite to detect breaking changes in clients. BreakGuard statically extracts every client method (focal method) that invokes the target library method (call site), then generates tests per focal method. A test detects a BC if it passes on the pre-breaking version and fails on the breaking version. We evaluate our approach on 89 real-world breaking changes from the BUMP dataset, using 3 LLMs (GPT4o, Qwen3-coder-480B, GPT-OSS-120B) and three context levels: minimal, method, and class. Using the best-performing configuration, BreakGuard detects 30.3% of breaking changes (27 of 89) at a mean cost of roughly $0.90 USD per detected breaking change. We successfully detected BCs from different library categories (e.g., JSON libraries, logging, parsing), but we find LLM-generated tests to be more reliable for detecting crash-type breaking changes as opposed to behavioural BCs.

</details>


### [216] [From Agent Behaviour to Agent-Friendly Documentation: An Empirical Study of How Coding Agents Discover, Read, and Write Technical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20195)
*Zhijun Gao,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过两个公开数据集（557个编码会话和33,097个agentic pull request）对自主编码代理与文档的交互行为进行了实证研究，发现代理主要依赖面向代理的工件（指令文件和备注占60.5%），而传统技术文档和API参考仅占少数；文档咨询与代码编辑的关联在调整模型下显著（OR 1.33），但文档咨询与实际测试负相关（OR 0.39）；提出"双叶循环"而非线性路径的描述性交互模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的软件变更由自主编码代理完成，但代理会查阅哪些文档、何时查阅以及查阅后产生什么后果仍然是未知的，这挑战了当前文档编写实践的基础。

Method: 采用行为学方法，对两个公共数据集进行事件级分析：SWE-chat的557个代理编码会话（94,813个开发事件，含3,033次文档交互）和AIDev的33,097个代理pull request（690,260个文件级变更记录），使用相邻转移概率、lift值及阶段调整模型（OR）等统计方法进行量化分析。

Result: 四项核心发现：1）代理文档工作以面向代理的工件为主（60.5% vs 传统文档10.6%、API参考1.3%）；2）文档咨询与代码编辑的关联不明确（相邻转移概率0.002，调整后OR 1.33）；3）未观察到显式基于文档的验证序列，文档咨询与即时测试负相关（lift 0.23，OR 0.39）；4）咨询主要是自发性的（70.2%）而非失败驱动的（7.5%），且文档变更普遍滞后于代码变更（4.7倍）。

Conclusion: 提出代理-文档交互的"双叶循环"描述性模型取代线性路径假设，并证明文档"可操作性"和"可验证性"这两个被广泛预设的"代理友好"属性缺乏一致的行为支持；同时发布研究管线、编码方案和事件级数据。

Abstract: Technical documentation is written for human developers, but an increasing share of software changes is now authored by autonomous coding agents. Which documents they consult, when, and what follows remain unknown. We conduct a behaviour-grounded study of agent-documentation interaction across two public datasets: 557 agentic coding sessions from SWE-chat, yielding 94,813 development events including 3,033 documentation interactions; and 33,097 agentic pull requests from AIDev, with 690,260 classified file-level change records. Four findings challenge current documentation practice. First, agents' documentation work is dominated by agent-facing artefacts: instruction files and working notes account for 60.5% of all documentation interactions, versus 10.6% for classical technical documentation and 1.3% for API references. Second, the link between consultation and code editing is unresolved: the adjacent transition probability is 0.002 and the unadjusted three-event lift 1.05, whereas a stage-adjusted model places it above unity (OR 1.33 [1.09, 1.62]); documentation creation is elevated unadjusted (lift 1.67) but its adjusted interval includes unity. Third, no explicit documentation-based validation sequence was observed, and consultation is associated with less immediate testing (lift 0.23, cluster CI 0.08-0.45; adjusted OR 0.39 [0.25, 0.60]).
